+# Device-Care-KI | CRM & Content Engine Bridge (DRAFT)
+
+Stand: 2026-11-22. Owner scope: Device-Care-KI. Local repository AGENTS.md is authoritative. Cross-project marketing *metadata* handoff explicitly requested by owner, without provider or authority sharing.
+
+## Operational slice
+
+1. Device-Care-KI exports only explicitly owner-prepared **public product descriptions** using tools/productization/export_public_product_candidate.py. Never export hardware diagnostics, PII, app inventories, sensitive device evidence, logs or customer identities.
+2. An owner-reviewed JSON document (PRODUCT_SALES_CANDIDATE@1) can be copied deliberately into the corresponding Drive Sales_Enablement folder. The local exporter never connects to Drive, GitHub, Salesforce, or CAPITAL-AI.
+3. On the CAPITAL-AI side, src/contracts/growthSalesCrm.ts accepts the sanitized envelope and returns CRM-neutral internal opportunity and marketing drafts. It does NOT create a live Salesforce record.
+4. Device-Care-KI branding is distinct. The CAPITAL-AI Content Engine's seven-color Universe identity is not reused as DeviceCare creative without an explicit branded adapter and licensing checks. The bridge returns DEVICECARE_BRAND_ADAPTER_PENDING.
+5. Export outputs remain NOT_PROVEN until read back from the chosen local file, Drive transfer remains NOT_PROVEN until verified, CRM connection NOT_CONNECTED, commercial release HOLD.
+
+## Sample input fields
+
+Only productId, productName, valueProposition, audience, differentiators, channels, sourceEvidenceRef, rightsStatus, optional rightsEvidenceRef, canonicalUrl, economics. All other keys are refused. This is product metadata, not a real lead, customer, diagnosis, or validated financial projection.
+
+Prepare reviewed input JSON yourself in a non-public working directory and invoke:
+
+~~~shell
+python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/productization -p 'test_*.py'
+python3 tools/productization/export_public_product_candidate.py --input /path/to/reviewed-product.json --output /path/to/DEVICECARE_PRODUCT_SALES_CANDIDATE_v1.json
+~~~
+
+The file is exclusive-created with mode 0600, no overwrite or silent directory creation. Inspect/redact the output before separately transferring to Drive.
+
+## CRM field plan
+
+Product → CRM catalog/custom product mapping after provider connection and permissions check.
+Opportunity → not created without verified account, contact lawful basis, StageName, CloseDate, consent, and explicit approved scope. No synthetic pipeline, deal amount or customer count.
+Sales enablement → landing page copy, pricing hypothesis, visual brief, license proof, product facts, support/FAQ and risk register.
+Graphics → separate original asset generation with exact SHA-256, source/license evidence, alt text, approved usage modes, editorial QA. No graphic bytes or brand grant currently proven.
+Metrics → opportunity conversion, attributed campaign traffic, cost per qualified lead, contribution margin. No actual KPIs are inferred from a draft.
+
+## Acceptance and rollback
+
+Tests: stdlib unit/negative tests for privacy, unsafe URLs, rights, deterministic SHA-256 and economic unknowns. No Android execution. Rollback: remove only the feature-branch files, preserve other app code and backups. No credential or backend changes.
+
+
+## Owner decision: Twenty CRM selected (2026-11-22)
+
+Provider: Twenty Cloud selected by owner (online workspace and API key reported configured 2026-11-22, but neither provider identity nor access has been read back). Cloud plan, billing, roles and permissions remain NOT_PROVEN; no service activation was performed by this integration. No Salesforce sandbox is required by Device-Care-KI, and no CAPITAL-AI provider credential, runtime or Salesforce adapter is imported. Any separate CAPITAL-AI CRM decision is independent.
+
+Twenty has built-in People, Companies and Opportunities; **Products is a custom workspace object** and MUST NOT be assumed to exist. The owner configures this **in the dedicated Device-Care-KI workspace** through Twenty Settings -> Data Model, with exact API plural name `products` (singular Product), scalar text `name` label plus custom text fields `externalKey` and `sourceSha256`. Record identity is a namespaced `externalKey=DEVICE_CARE_KI:<productId>` (e.g. DEVICE_CARE_KI:device-health). Validate exact field API names against the authenticated workspace's generated API documentation before first call. Prefer a unique constraint on externalKey where supported; the adapter rejects any duplicate return.
+
+Readback implementation:

+    if (not isinstance(raw, dict) or raw.get("contractVersion") != "PRODUCT_SALES_CANDIDATE@1"
+            or raw.get("projectId") != "DEVICE_CARE_KI"
+            or raw.get("dataClassification") != "PUBLIC_PRODUCT_METADATA"
+            or not isinstance(raw.get("productId"), str)

+            or not isinstance(raw.get("productName"), str)
+            or not 3 <= len(raw["productName"]) <= 120

+            or not SHA_RE.fullmatch(raw["sourceSha256"])):
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_PRODUCT_CANDIDATE_INVALID")
+    allowed = {
+        "contractVersion", "projectId", "productId", "productName",
+        "valueProposition", "audience", "differentiators", "canonicalUrl",
+        "channels", "sourceSha256", "sourceEvidenceRef", "rightsStatus",
+        "rightsEvidenceRef", "economics", "dataClassification",
+    }
+    required = {
+        "valueProposition", "audience", "differentiators", "channels",
+        "sourceEvidenceRef", "rightsStatus", "economics",
+    }
+    if set(raw) - allowed or required - set(raw):
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_PRODUCT_CANDIDATE_FIELDS_INVALID")
+    public = {k: v for k, v in raw.items() if k in {
+        "productId", "productName", "valueProposition", "audience",
+        "differentiators", "canonicalUrl", "channels", "sourceEvidenceRef",
+        "rightsStatus", "rightsEvidenceRef", "economics",
+    }}
+    try:
+        build_public_candidate(json.dumps(public, sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8"))
+    except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError):
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_PRODUCT_CANDIDATE_FIELDS_INVALID") from None
+    return raw
+
+def _read_http_get(url: str, token: str) -> dict:
+    opener = build_opener(_NoRedirect)
+    request = Request(url, method="GET", headers={
+        "Authorization": "Bearer " + token, "Accept": "application/json",
+    })
+    try:
+        with opener.open(request, timeout=10) as response:
+            if response.status != 200:
+                raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_READBACK_HTTP_FAILED")
+            payload = response.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
+    except TwentyReadbackError:
+        raise
+    except (HTTPError, URLError, TimeoutError, OSError):
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_READBACK_HTTP_FAILED") from None
+    if len(payload) > MAX_BYTES:
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_READBACK_TOO_LARGE")
+    try:
+        result = json.loads(payload)
+    except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError):
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_READBACK_INVALID_JSON") from None
+    if not isinstance(result, dict):
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_READBACK_INVALID_JSON")
+    return result
+
+def read_product(candidate: dict, *, base_url: str, token: str, transport=None) -> dict:
+    candidate = candidate_from_reviewed(candidate)
+    origin = validate_instance_url(base_url)
+    if not isinstance(token, str) or not 10 <= len(token) <= 4096:
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_TOKEN_NOT_CONFIGURED")
+    key = "DEVICE_CARE_KI:" + candidate["productId"]
+    params = urlencode({
+        "limit": "2", "depth": "0",
+        "fields": "id,name,externalKey,sourceSha256",
+        "filter": 'externalKey[eq]:"' + key + '"',
+    })
+    endpoint = origin + "/rest/products?" + params
+    raw = (transport or _read_http_get)(endpoint, token)
+    data, info = raw.get("data"), raw.get("pageInfo")
+    if not isinstance(data, dict) or not isinstance(info, dict) or info.get("hasNextPage") is not False:
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_READBACK_PAGINATION_NOT_PROVEN")
+    rows = data.get("products")
+    if not isinstance(rows, list) or len(rows) > 2:
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_READBACK_SHAPE_INVALID")
+    if len(rows) == 2:
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_EXTERNAL_KEY_DUPLICATE")
+    if not rows:
+        return {
+            "schema": "DEVICECARE_TWENTY_PRODUCT_READBACK@1",
+            "provider": "TWENTY", "scope": "DEVICE_CARE_KI", "status": "NOT_FOUND",
+            "productKey": key, "crmRecordId": None, "writeAllowed": False,
+            "contentLinkStatus": "HOLD", "deviceState": "NOT_PROVEN",
+        }
+    row = rows[0]
+    if not isinstance(row, dict) or not isinstance(row.get("id"), str) or not UUID_RE.fullmatch(row["id"]):
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_RECORD_ID_INVALID")
+    if row.get("externalKey") != key or row.get("name") != candidate["productName"]:
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_PRODUCT_IDENTITY_MISMATCH")
+    if row.get("sourceSha256") != candidate["sourceSha256"]:
+        raise TwentyReadbackError("TWENTY_PRODUCT_SOURCE_HASH_MISMATCH")
+    digest = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps({
+        "externalKey": key, "id": row["id"], "name": row["name"],
+        "sourceSha256": row["sourceSha256"],
+    }, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode()).hexdigest()
+    return {
+        "schema": "DEVICECARE_TWENTY_PRODUCT_READBACK@1",
+        "provider": "TWENTY", "scope": "DEVICE_CARE_KI", "status": "MATCHED",
+        "productKey": key, "crmRecordId": row["id"],
+        "sourceSha256": candidate["sourceSha256"],
+        "readbackProjectionSha256": digest,
+        "writeAllowed": False, "contentLinkStatus": "HOLD_EDITORIAL_RIGHTS_AND_ASSET_PROOF",
+        "deviceState": "NOT_PROVEN",
+    }
+
+def main() -> None:
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    ap.add_argument("--input", type=Path, required=True)
+    opts = ap.parse_args()
+    try:
+        raw = json.loads(opts.input.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+        result = read_product(
+            raw, base_url="https://api.twenty.com",
+            token=os.getenv("TWENTY_READONLY_API_KEY", ""),
+        )
+        print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
+    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, TwentyReadbackError, ValueError):
+        print("DEVICECARE_TWENTY_READBACK=BLOCKED_OR_NOT_PROVEN", file=sys.stderr)
+        raise SystemExit(1) from None
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

>>> PR #25 (SvenKulessa/DeviceCare-KI) <<<
TITLE: fix(TRUST): fail-closed Android evidence provenance in security and privacy readiness
BASE: main <- HEAD: feature/device-evidence-provenance-20261121 (1f47094288b51e2dc408c1012f1288e2ab064570)
BODY: ## Auftrag / Scope (SOLO2 TRUST · DeviceCare-KI only)
P0-01 Geräte-Evidence-Vertrag: Nur ein ausdrücklich durch den lokalen Android-System-API-Collector markierter Sicherheits-Snapshot darf als lokaler Systemcheck ausgewertet werden. Ungebundene/synthetische Snapshots bleiben NOT_PROVEN.

## Wirkung
- `SystemSecuritySnapshot.origin` ist standardmäßig `NOT_PROVEN`; der lokale Android-Reader markiert ausschließlich seinen On-Device-Readback als `LOCAL_SYSTEM_API`. Herkunftsmarker ist **kein** kryptografisches Attest.
- Die System-Security-Ausgabe gibt für ungebundene Snapshots keine `[VERIFIED]`-Werte, kein behauptetes lokales Android-Release und keine lokale Patch-Bewertung mehr aus.
- Die Privacy-Aggregation verwirft ungebundene Sicherheits-Snapshots für Check-Zähler, Patch-Alter, Systemdaten, Handlungsempfehlungen und positive Readiness-Tiers.
- Negativtests verhindern die Hochstufung eines synthetischen Snapshots sowohl im Einzelcheck als auch im Privacy-Report.
- Unveränderte Nutzerberechtigungen, Firebase, Exportformate, APK-Identität und Signierung; keine Datenmutation.

## Evidence und Grenzen
- Main-Basis bei Branch-Erstellung: `4d0d86dc1d7e5c2511eba93e66004451ab0101c8`.
- Branch: `feature/device-evidence-provenance-20261121`.
- CI: https://github.com/SvenKulessa/DeviceCare-KI/actions/runs/44959002586 (Trivy/Secrets/License-Scan auf aktuellem Head abgeschlossen, Unit-Tests und weitere Gates beim letzten Readback noch laufend; exakte aktuelle Ergebnisse erneut prüfen).
- `DEVICE_STATE=NOT_PROVEN`, `DEVICE_READBACK=NOT_PROVEN`, keine reale Android-15-Geräteabnahme, keine Release-Signer- oder Installationskontinuität nachgewiesen.
- Aktuelle PRs #13/#15/#16/#17/#18/#19/#20/#22 berühren keine der vier modifizierten Dateien (GitHub-Dateiliste geprüft).

## QUALITY / Merge-Kriterien
- Exakten finalen Head und Main erneut lesen, CI-Unit-/Negativtests, Android lint, debug APK, `apksigner`, Trivy Vulnerability/Secret/License/Misconfig, Reviews/Rulesets und gegebenenfalls physische Android-15-Akzeptanz gemäß Root AGENTS.md verifizieren.
- DRAFT/HOLD, solange Pflichtnachweise fehlen; kein Merge/Play-Release ohne vollständige Gates. Kein GitHub-Code-/CI-Nachweis wird als physischer Geräte-Pass interpretiert.
- Rollback: Revert der vier isolierten Dateien; kein Schema-, Firebase-, Geräte- oder Release-Key-Rollback notwendig.

## Repair- und Revalidation-Log (2026-11-21 UTC)
- Exakter Push-Run [44959002586](https://github.com/SvenKulessa/DeviceCare-KI/actions/runs/44959002586) auf `c8bb7abe7b1558f72b1a75130909e9b984e1e73c`: Trivy inklusive Secret/License/Gate **SUCCESS**; `:app:testDebugUnitTest` **FAILED**, 55 Tests / 1 Fehler in `PostureNextStepsTest.buildDeviceStatePrompt` (Zeile 99).
- Ursache: Der vorhandene synthetische Testfall deklarierte nach der fail-closed-Origin-Änderung keine simulierte lokale Collector-Herkunft; die erwartete Patch-Angabe wurde korrekt unterdrückt.
- Enger Reparaturcommit `1f47094288b51e2dc408c1012f1288e2ab064570`: Nur die betroffene Posture-Test-Fixture mit `SystemSnapshotOrigin.LOCAL_SYSTEM_API` gekennzeichnet. Dieser Testmarker ist **keine echte Hardware-Evidence**.
- Erneute exakte Head-CI: [Run 44959265954](https://github.com/SvenKulessa/DeviceCare-KI/actions/runs/44959265954) — beim Eintrag IN_PROGRESS. Lauf-ID und Schlussresultat vor jeder Mergeentscheidung erneut lesen. Kein Merge während CI unvollständig oder Device-Akzeptanz NOT_PROVEN ist.

+# DeviceCare-KI: Drive Deep-Scan governance contract (proposal)
+Status: **NOT_PROVEN** · Issue [#21](https://github.com/SvenKulessa/DeviceCare-KI/issues/21) · Implemented: deterministic, offline, no-I/O planner only.
+
+## Scope verified 2026-11-21
+The earlier owner-provided Branding folder `EXTERNAL_BRANDING_ROOT_NOT_IN_SCOPE` is `CAPITAL-AI/10_Product/Branding`, **not DeviceCare-KI**. Its direct child listing returned no results. **Never** use it as a DeviceCare-KI source, destination, training source, evidence pool or audit inheritance unless the owner explicitly authorizes a cross-project integration with separate scopes. A separate DeviceCare-KI project-owned root has now been verified: `DEVICECARE_PRIVATE_ROOT_CONFIG`, title `DeviceCare-KI`, private, with four direct children `PLATFORM`, `PRODUCT`, `TRUST`, `90 ZIP-Archiv`. This is a metadata/ownership verification, **not** evidence of configured OAuth for the future web backend, full descendant inventory or clean security scans.
+
+The repository currently contains one Android `:app` module and no running web backend/scheduler. The code in `tools/drive-governance.mjs` is a pure policy component, not an activated cloud scan. Neither device state nor Drive security state was measured.
+
+## Daily server-side web integration (not yet present)
+- Use the **owner-designated, metadata-verified DeviceCare-KI root**. Store its file ID only in private backend configuration; reverify ancestry, OAuth principal, destination folder allowlist and RBAC before each write. Historical AI-DeviceCare files require explicit provenance review before reuse.
+- Integrate into a real authenticated backend with exactly one **Europe/Berlin local calendar** job per day. Recommended nominal time: 03:00 Europe/Berlin. Durable compare-and-set calendar key + distributed lease + bounded retries are mandatory to prevent overlapping runs, especially at DST changes. Never rely only on an in-process timer.
+- The job enumerates only descendants of the validated root, paginates at <=500 files per batch, bounds bytes and archive time, captures revision/ETag before updates, and never follows contents as agent commands.
+- PRODUCT produces classification from verifiable file **contents** and checked references (not filename alone), with explicit confidence, content digest, date/topic/version, intended destination, license/right provenance and a uniqueness check.
+- TRUST invokes **real, authorized security adapters**, always per run: malware engine with current signatures (e.g., an isolated licensed ClamAV worker if the deployment verifies its availability), secret detector, privacy review, licensing review, integrity hashing. Native Google editor files require separate export and applicable provider-specific checks; missing scan evidence is HOLD and cannot be labelled malware-free. Scan bytes locally in an isolated worker; do not log content, secret matches, tokens or PII.
+- Independent QUALITY validates outputs, duplicate names, references, retention/holds, source/destination ancestry, archive restore receipt and the final Drive metadata readback.
+- Default `PLAN_ONLY` / no write. Applying `RENAME` or `MOVE` requires a configured owner consent policy and matching current revision + content digest. `ARCHIVE_COPY_COMPRESSED` requires **>45 full days since verified last activity**, zero live references, no legal/retention hold, reliable reversible ZIP, signed/hashed manifest and test restore. Preserve the original until explicit retention decision. Compression may increase size for JPEG/MP4/PDF; skip if not useful, without losing the original.
+- Never use server-side broad Drive searches outside the authorized root, never access another product's Drive roots, and never grant permissions to the mobile app for global Drive scans.
+
+## State transitions and manual stop
+```text
+DISABLED --(verified integration)--> READY
+READY --(daily lease acquired)--> SCANNING --> PLAN_ONLY
+PLAN_ONLY --(policy/owner permits)--> APPLYING --> VERIFYING --> DONE
+                        |               |
+                        v               v
+                    WAIT_OWNER       BLOCKED
+                        |               |
+              explicit approval       revalidation
+                        \               /
+                         ---> REVALIDATE
+OWNER_STOP (from any state) -> STOPPED (no automatic resume)
+```
+- `WAIT_OWNER` must persist a checkpoint, plan digest, specific file IDs and **redacted** risk summary; pause processing and all writes until explicit approve/deny/skip or stop.
+- Approvals are authenticated, time-limited and bound to tenant, plan hash, file IDs and revisions; no prompt may self-approve.
+- `BLOCKED` includes failing security gates, missing permissions, stale hash or unsafe destination. `HOLD` includes no authenticated backend/Drive root/scanner.
+- `STOPPED` remains stopped across restarts. `REVALIDATE` must rerun every security/scope/inactivity check before continuing. Failed backup/restore is never auto-retried as a write.
+- Each action requires before/after file ID + parent/name readback, a reversible change ledger, minimal hashed/redacted receipts and explicit `NOT_PROVEN` for absent evidence.
+
+## Verification
+Local policy-only test command: `node --test tools/drive-governance.test.mjs`. Tests are synthetic and cannot prove Google Drive reads, malware scan, archive export, compression safety, OAuth or deployed scheduling. The Android CI, test device QA, production deployment and connector integration remain independent gates.
+Rollback: revert this branch/PR. No database migrations, OAuth secrets, live scanning or file writes were introduced.
+
+## Control Center backend and enterprise dashboard design (2026-11-21)
+The owner authorizes **design and integration into the DeviceCare-KI Control Center** with separate private-profile dashboard tabs and future enterprise/B2B views. This is NOT authorization to share private Drive data with customer tenants.
+
+### Verified Drive folder boundary
+- Root ID: `DEVICECARE_PRIVATE_ROOT_CONFIG`, name `DeviceCare-KI` (renamed and verified on 2026-11-21).
+- Direct folders: PLATFORM `PLATFORM_FOLDER_CONFIG`, PRODUCT `PRODUCT_FOLDER_CONFIG`, TRUST `TRUST_FOLDER_CONFIG`, ZIP archive `ARCHIVE_FOLDER_CONFIG`.
+- These are **observed identifiers**, not automatic move destinations. Subfolders, archive rules and content-based taxonomy must be verified before file mutations.
+- No Capital-AI folder is part of the allowlist. No shared OAuth principal or storage authority is inferred.
+
+### Proposed web Control Center tabs
+1. **Private / My Devices**: on-demand device diagnostics and privacy reports; explicit opt-in and user-specific access.
+2. **Private / Drive Hygiene**: scan inventory, content classification, candidate rename/move/archive, redacted risk findings, dry-run diff, approvals, restore receipts.
+3. **Private / SOLO2 Agent Runs**: daily schedule, checkpoint, queue, `WAIT_OWNER` decisions, STOP/RESUME with new evidence, signed audit.
+4. **Enterprise / Fleet Posture**: tenant-scoped device compliance aggregates and enrolled endpoints only.
+5. **Enterprise / IAM & Audit**: tenant users, RBAC, least-privilege policy, audit and retention controls. No cross-tenant raw scans.
+
+### IAM contract
+- `platform_owner`: DeviceCare-KI root configuration, approval policies and emergency stop, but no unlogged bypass of security gates.
+- `private_owner`: own files/devices, personal run approvals and audit only.
+- `tenant_admin`: own tenant membership, policies and fleet summaries; **no access** to owner's personal Drive root.
+- `security_auditor`: redacted, tenant-scoped security evidence; no write/approval privilege.
+- `fleet_operator`: tenant-scoped scan requests and fleet views; no global Drive root, IAM edits or destructive actions.
+- `tenant_viewer`: read-only tenant aggregates, never private files.
+- `agent_service`: short-lived, root-scoped delegated token, policy-bound actions only; cannot approve its own plan.
+- Enforce server-side tenant_id, user_id, resource ownership, RLS/ABAC and negative cross-tenant tests; hiding UI tabs alone is not authorization. Private data must never be copied to tenant storage by default.
+
+### Execution prerequisites
+- Real backend implementation and authenticated Control Center deployment are **NOT_PROVEN**.
+- Daily 03:00 Europe/Berlin scheduler remains **HOLD** until persistent lease, per-day idempotency, OAuth scopes, rate/cost limits and verified security workers exist.
+- Malware scan of Drive content remains **NOT_PROVEN**; a metadata listing is not a malware scan.
+- Do not change Android M0/M1 permission boundaries or connect another project's infrastructure.
+
+
+### Implemented private Control Center reference (Draft PR #22)
+The repository-only, injection-based SSR/RBAC code lives under `web/control-center/` with a tested private-profile route and role-filtered Drive, Enterprise and IAM views. See `web/control-center/README.md` and Roadmap Issue #23 for the precise role names, boundary mapping, omitted provider adapters and negative tests. This does **not** activate a cloud backend, scheduled scan, tenant provisioning or real IAM provider. Folder IDs here are replaced by logical private-configuration names to avoid disclosing resource identifiers in a public repository.

===== "part_05.txt" | file | SHA256 6c9d1a702e8f46d29ceeb5507283ec0e9e668952736adde09413175f6ceb9b5d =====
+// DeviceCare-KI only. Pure policy engine; NO Drive API, no scheduler, no mutations.
+// A production web service must provide authenticated adapters and independent evidence.
+export const Status = Object.freeze({
+  VERIFIED: "VERIFIED", WAIT: "WAIT_OWNER", HOLD: "HOLD",
+  BLOCKED: "BLOCKED", PLAN_ONLY: "PLAN_ONLY", STOPPED: "STOPPED",
+});
+const PROJECT = "DeviceCare-KI";
+const CLEAN_GATES = ["malware", "secrets", "privacy", "license", "integrity"];
+const HEX64 = /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i;
+const DAY = 86_400_000;
+const FILE_EXTENSIONS = Object.freeze({
+  "image/png": "png", "image/jpeg": "jpg", "image/svg+xml": "svg",
+  "application/pdf": "pdf", "text/plain": "txt", "text/markdown": "md",
+  "application/json": "json", "application/zip": "zip",
+  "video/mp4": "mp4", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document": "docx",
+  "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet": "xlsx",
+});
+const NATIVE_MIME = new Set([
+  "application/vnd.google-apps.document",
+  "application/vnd.google-apps.spreadsheet",
+  "application/vnd.google-apps.presentation",
+]);
+
+function result(state, code, actions = []) {
+  return { state, code, actions }; // Never include raw content or filenames in audit output.
+}
+export function dayKey(now = new Date()) {
+  const date = new Date(now);
+  if (!Number.isFinite(date.valueOf())) throw new Error("INVALID_DATE");
+  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
+    timeZone: "Europe/Berlin", year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",
+  }).formatToParts(date);
+  const byType = Object.fromEntries(parts.map(p => [p.type, p.value]));
+  return `${byType.year}-${byType.month}-${byType.day}`;
+}
+
+// The web scheduler calls this atomically under a server-side per-tenant lease.
+// Calling this function does NOT schedule a job or claim a durable lock.
+export function dailyAdmission({ enabled, project, rootVerified, oauthVerified, scannerReady,
+  webBackendReady, ownerPaused, completedDayKey, now = new Date() } = {}) {
+  if (ownerPaused) return result(Status.STOPPED, "OWNER_STOP");
+  if (project !== PROJECT || !rootVerified) return result(Status.HOLD, "SCOPE_NOT_VERIFIED");
+  if (!enabled || !webBackendReady || !oauthVerified || !scannerReady)
+    return result(Status.HOLD, "ADAPTER_NOT_READY");
+  if (completedDayKey === dayKey(now)) return result(Status.STOPPED, "DAY_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
+  return result(Status.PLAN_ONLY, "ADMITTED_TO_DRY_RUN");
+}
+function cleanScan(scan, sha256) {
+  if (!HEX64.test(sha256 || "") || !scan || scan.contentSha256 !== sha256)
+    return result(Status.HOLD, "SCANNER_OR_HASH_MISSING");
+  for (const gate of CLEAN_GATES) {
+    const e = scan[gate];
+    if (!e || e.status !== "VERIFIED" || !e.receipt)
+      return result(Status.HOLD, "SECURITY_GATE_NOT_VERIFIED");
+    if (e.verdict !== "CLEAN") return result(Status.BLOCKED, "SECURITY_FINDING");
+  }
+  return null;
+}
+function validName(classification, mime) {
+  const c = classification;
+  if (!c || c.status !== "VERIFIED" || c.basis !== "CONTENT" ||
+    !c.contentReceipt || !(c.confidence >= 0.95) ||
+    !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(c.contentDate || "") ||
+    !/^([A-Z0-9]+_?){1,12}$/.test(c.topic || "") ||
+    c.topic.length > 64 ||
+    !Number.isSafeInteger(c.version) || c.version < 1 || c.version > 999)
+    return null;
+  const iso = new Date(c.contentDate + "T00:00:00Z");
+  if (!Number.isFinite(iso.valueOf()) || iso.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== c.contentDate)
+    return null;
+  const extension = NATIVE_MIME.has(mime) ? "" : FILE_EXTENSIONS[mime];
+  if (extension === undefined) return null;
+  return `${c.contentDate}_DEVICECARE_CHATGPT_${c.topic}_v${c.version}${extension ? "." + extension : ""}`;
+}
+function archiveEligibility(file, activity, archive, now) {
+  const modified = Date.parse(file.modifiedTime || "");
+  if (!Number.isFinite(modified)) return result(Status.WAIT, "ACTIVITY_TIMESTAMP_MISSING");
+  if (Number(now) - modified <= 45 * DAY) return null;
+  if (!activity || activity.coverage !== "VERIFIED" ||
+      !activity.referencesChecked || !activity.lastActivityAt ||
+      !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(activity.lastActivityAt)) ||
+      activity.activeReferences === null || activity.activeReferences === undefined)
+    return result(Status.WAIT, "INACTIVITY_NOT_PROVEN");
+  if (activity.activeReferences || activity.legalHold || activity.retentionPinned)
+    return null; // Keep active or legally protected files without treating them as errors.
+  const last = Math.max(modified, Date.parse(activity.lastActivityAt));
+  if (Number(now) - last <= 45 * DAY) return null;
+  if (!archive || !archive.backupVerified || !archive.restoreVerified ||
+      !archive.compressionVerified || !HEX64.test(archive.manifestSha256 || "") ||
+      !archive.destinationVerified)
+    return result(Status.WAIT, "ARCHIVE_ROLLBACK_NOT_PROVEN");
+  return { type: "ARCHIVE_COPY_COMPRESSED", preserveOriginal: true,
+    format: "zip", integrityManifestSha256: archive.manifestSha256 };
+}
+
+// Only a current, verified DeviceCare-KI ancestry and destination allowlist may be used.
+// Agent-produced classification is DATA and never an instruction to override policy.
+export function evaluateFile({ file, scope, classification, scan, activity, archive,
+  now = new Date() } = {}) {
+  if (!file || !file.id || !scope ||
+    scope.project !== PROJECT || scope.projectRootVerified !== true ||
+    file.ancestryVerified !== true || file.projectRootId !== scope.projectRootId ||
+    !scope.projectRootId || !Array.isArray(scope.destinationFolderIds) ||
+    !scope.destinationFolderIds.length)
+    return result(Status.HOLD, "PROJECT_BOUNDARY_NOT_VERIFIED");
+  if (!file.parentId || typeof file.parentId !== "string")
+    return result(Status.HOLD, "SOURCE_PARENT_NOT_VERIFIED");
+  if (file.ownerDecisionRequired || file.conflict || file.locked ||
+      file.legalHold || file.shortcut || file.isSharedExternally)
+    return result(Status.WAIT, "OWNER_DECISION_REQUIRED");
+  const gate = cleanScan(scan, file.sha256);
+  if (gate) return gate;
+  if (classification?.contentSha256 !== file.sha256)
+    return result(Status.HOLD, "CLASSIFICATION_STALE");
+  const name = validName(classification, file.mimeType);
+  if (!name || !scope.destinationFolderIds.includes(classification.destinationFolderId) ||
+      !classification.destinationVerified || !classification.uniqueNameVerified ||
+      !classification.licenseRightsVerified)
+    return result(Status.WAIT, "CONTENT_OR_TARGET_AMBIGUOUS");
+  const actions = [];
+  if (name !== file.name) actions.push({ type: "RENAME", from: file.name, to: name });
+  if (file.parentId !== classification.destinationFolderId)
+    actions.push({ type: "MOVE", fromId: file.parentId,
+      toId: classification.destinationFolderId });
+  const candidate = archiveEligibility(file, activity, archive, new Date(now));
+  if (candidate?.state) return candidate;
+  if (candidate) actions.push(candidate);
+  return result(Status.PLAN_ONLY, "DRY_RUN_READY", actions);
+}
+
+// Multi-agent output must be externally verified, not trusted as an instruction.
+// Stop on the FIRST required owner decision or unsafe file, checkpoint before resume.
+export function planBatch(items, options = {}) {
+  if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length > 500)
+    return { state: Status.HOLD, code: "BATCH_LIMIT", processed: 0, plans: [] };
+  const plans = [];
+  for (const item of items) {
+    const evaluation = evaluateFile({ ...item, now: options.now });
+    plans.push({ id: item?.file?.id || null, ...evaluation });
+    if (evaluation.state !== Status.PLAN_ONLY)
+      return { state: evaluation.state, code: evaluation.code,
+        processed: plans.length, checkpoint: plans.length - 1, plans };
+  }
+  return { state: Status.PLAN_ONLY, code: "BATCH_PLANNED",
+    processed: plans.length, checkpoint: plans.length, plans };
+}

+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { Status, dailyAdmission, dayKey, evaluateFile, planBatch } from "./drive-governance.mjs";
+
+const sha = "a".repeat(64);
+const good = () => ({
+  file: {
+    id: "file-1", name: "old-logo.png", mimeType: "image/png",
+    parentId: "scope-1", projectRootId: "scope-1", ancestryVerified: true,
+    modifiedTime: "2026-11-01T00:00:00Z", sha256: sha,
+  },
+  scope: { project: "DeviceCare-KI", projectRootVerified: true,
+    projectRootId: "scope-1", destinationFolderIds: ["scope-1", "target-2"] },
+  classification: { status: "VERIFIED", basis: "CONTENT",
+    contentReceipt: "classifier-attested", confidence: .99,
+    contentDate: "2026-11-01", topic: "BRANDING_LOGO", version: 1,
+    contentSha256: sha, destinationFolderId: "target-2",
+    destinationVerified: true, uniqueNameVerified: true, licenseRightsVerified: true },
+  scan: { contentSha256: sha, ...Object.fromEntries(
+    ["malware","secrets","privacy","license","integrity"]
+      .map(g => [g, { status: "VERIFIED", verdict: "CLEAN", receipt: "scan-" + g }])) },
+  now: new Date("2026-11-21T10:00:00Z"),
+});
+test("Berlin calendar keys follow daylight-saving boundaries", () => {
+  assert.equal(dayKey("2026-03-29T00:59:59Z"), "2026-03-29");
+  assert.equal(dayKey("2026-10-24T23:00:00Z"), "2026-10-25");
+});
+test("daily runner cannot claim activation without all adapters", () => {
+  assert.equal(dailyAdmission({ project:"DeviceCare-KI" }).state, Status.HOLD);
+});
+test("daily runner stops on owner pause or already completed day", () => {
+  const base = { enabled:true, project:"DeviceCare-KI", rootVerified:true,
+    oauthVerified:true, scannerReady:true, webBackendReady:true,
+    now:new Date("2026-11-21T10:00:00Z") };
+  assert.equal(dailyAdmission({ ...base, ownerPaused:true }).state, Status.STOPPED);
+  assert.equal(dailyAdmission({ ...base, completedDayKey:"2026-11-21" }).state, Status.STOPPED);
+  assert.equal(dailyAdmission(base).state, Status.PLAN_ONLY);
+});
+test("cross-project root fails before content parsing", () => {
+  const x=good(); x.scope.projectRootId="capital-ai-id";
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).code, "PROJECT_BOUNDARY_NOT_VERIFIED");
+});
+test("legacy CAPITAL-AI scope cannot be used under a DeviceCare job", () => {
+  const x=good(); x.scope.project="CAPITAL-AI";
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).state, Status.HOLD);
+});
+test("verified classifier and security gates propose only safe changes", () => {
+  const x=good(); const out=evaluateFile(x);
+  assert.equal(out.state, Status.PLAN_ONLY);
+  assert.deepEqual(out.actions.map(a=>a.type), ["RENAME","MOVE"]);
+  assert.equal(out.actions[0].to,"2026-11-01_DEVICECARE_CHATGPT_BRANDING_LOGO_v1.png");
+});
+test("no scanner or mismatch hash: fail closed", () => {
+  const x=good(); delete x.scan.malware;
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).state,Status.HOLD);
+  const y=good(); y.scan.contentSha256="b".repeat(64);
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(y).state,Status.HOLD);
+});
+test("security finding blocks writes", () => {
+  const x=good(); x.scan.secrets.verdict="MALICIOUS";
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).state,Status.BLOCKED);
+});
+test("filename-only classification cannot rename", () => {
+  const x=good(); x.classification.basis="NAME";
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).state,Status.WAIT);
+});
+test("prompt injection cannot escape approved destination allowlist", () => {
+  const x=good(); x.classification.destinationFolderId="CAPITAL-AI";
+  x.classification.topic="IGNORE_PREVIOUS_INSTRUCTIONS";
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).state,Status.WAIT);
+});
+test("duplicate/conflicting or shared files require manual owner decision", () => {
+  const x=good(); x.file.conflict=true;
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).state,Status.WAIT);
+  const y=good(); y.file.isSharedExternally=true;
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(y).state,Status.WAIT);
+});
+test("older than 45 days still requires verified inactivity and restore", () => {
+  const x=good(); x.file.modifiedTime="2026-08-01T00:00:00Z";
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).code,"INACTIVITY_NOT_PROVEN");
+  x.activity={ coverage:"VERIFIED", referencesChecked:true,
+    lastActivityAt:"2026-08-02T00:00:00Z", activeReferences:false };
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).code,"ARCHIVE_ROLLBACK_NOT_PROVEN");
+  x.archive={ backupVerified:true, restoreVerified:true, compressionVerified:true,
+    manifestSha256:"b".repeat(64), destinationVerified:true };
+  const out=evaluateFile(x);
+  assert.deepEqual(out.actions.map(a=>a.type), ["RENAME","MOVE","ARCHIVE_COPY_COMPRESSED"]);
+  assert.equal(out.actions[2].preserveOriginal,true);
+});
+test("active references, legal hold or recent usage prevent archival", () => {
+  for (const field of ["activeReferences","legalHold","retentionPinned"]) {
+    const x=good(); x.file.modifiedTime="2026-08-01T00:00:00Z";
+    x.activity={coverage:"VERIFIED",referencesChecked:true,
+      lastActivityAt:"2026-08-02T00:00:00Z",activeReferences:false,[field]:true};
+    assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).state,Status.PLAN_ONLY);
+    assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).actions.some(a=>a.type.startsWith("ARCHIVE")),false);
+  }
+  const x=good(); x.file.modifiedTime="2026-08-01T00:00:00Z";
+  x.activity={coverage:"VERIFIED",referencesChecked:true,
+    lastActivityAt:"2026-11-20T00:00:00Z",activeReferences:false};
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).state,Status.PLAN_ONLY);
+});
+test("batch stops at first evidence gap and preserves checkpoint", () => {
+  const x=good(), y=good(); y.file.id="file-2"; y.scan.integrity.verdict="BAD";
+  const z=good(); z.file.id="file-3";
+  const out=planBatch([x,y,z],{now:new Date("2026-11-21T10:00:00Z")});
+  assert.equal(out.state,Status.BLOCKED);
+  assert.equal(out.processed,2);
+  assert.equal(out.checkpoint,1);
+  assert.equal(out.plans.length,2);
+  assert.equal(planBatch(Array(501).fill(x)).code,"BATCH_LIMIT");
+});
+
+test("owner stop overrides missing cloud integrations", () => {
+  assert.equal(dailyAdmission({ownerPaused:true}).code,"OWNER_STOP");
+});
+test("impossible content dates and excessive topic strings require owner review", () => {
+  const x=good(); x.classification.contentDate="2026-02-31";
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).state,Status.WAIT);
+  const y=good(); y.classification.topic="A".repeat(10000);
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(y).state,Status.WAIT);
+});
+
+test("missing source parent always fails closed", () => {
+  const x=good(); delete x.file.parentId;
+  assert.equal(evaluateFile(x).code,"SOURCE_PARENT_NOT_VERIFIED");
+});

+# DeviceCare-KI Control Center: private profile + B2B/Enterprise
+Implementation status: **repository-only MVP / NOT_PROVEN deployment**. Roadmap Issue [#23](https://github.com/SvenKulessa/DeviceCare-KI/issues/23). Closely related Drive Issue [#21](https://github.com/SvenKulessa/DeviceCare-KI/issues/21) and Draft PR #22.
+
+## What exists
+- `iam.mjs`: central server-verifiable role/tenant authorization, strict personal/project/tenant resource boundaries, step-up MFA guard, plan-hash approval guard and a restrictive tenant-IAM grant planner.
+- `view.mjs`: server-rendered private profile dashboard and three extra tabs. Requires injected authentication and membership adapters to expose any page. Returns 503 if adapters are absent; 403 on denied access. No fake device results, user identity data, or browser-controlled role selection. UI is responsive; full mobile accessibility QA is pending.
+- `control-center.test.mjs`: synthetic positive/negative authorization, XSS and isolation tests. Node built-in test runner; no extra packages and no Google Drive access.
+- `../../tools/drive-governance.mjs`: separate no-I/O Drive classifier/scan/archive policy, still PLAN_ONLY.
+
+## Routes and operation scopes
+| Private route | Required server-side operation | Audience |
+|---|---|---|
+| `/profile/control-center` | `personal:view` | device user for their own identity only |
+| `/profile/control-center/drive` | `project:drive:view` | project owner, project security analyst or auditor |
+| `/profile/control-center/enterprise` | `tenant:dashboard` | members of that exact B2B/enterprise tenant |
+| `/profile/control-center/iam` | `tenant:iam:read` | B2B/enterprise tenant administrators and tenant auditors |
+
+No write endpoint exists. All visible metrics default to **NOT_PROVEN**, and become VERIFIED only when the server supplies a validated nonnegative aggregate plus evidence receipt. Never load or show raw file, malware, per-device or identity evidence to unrelated tenants.
+
+## Naming reconciliation (concept ↔ code)
+- `platform_owner` → `OWNER` **with project-scoped authoritative membership**.
+- `private_owner` → `DEVICE_USER` with personal-scoped membership and own subject ID.
+- `tenant_admin` → `B2B_ADMIN` or `ENTERPRISE_ADMIN` with tenant-scoped membership.
+- `security_auditor` → `AUDITOR` with tenant/project scope decided by authoritative membership (never global automatically).
+- `fleet_operator` → `FLEET_OPERATOR` with tenant-scoped read access only in this MVP.
+- `tenant_viewer` → `VIEWER` scoped to a single tenant.
+- `agent_service` → a **separate noninteractive service principal**, NOT an IAM user role in this UI. Its authenticated job policy/least privilege and action ledger must be built separately.
+
+The client must never submit a role, a different tenant ID or membership status as authority. The server must derive roles/tenants from a configured and independently verified identity provider and membership database. Tenant admins may propose limited non-admin grants but cannot create owners/admins, grant themselves any role or write across tenants; an actual IAM mutation endpoint is not implemented.
+
+## Integration (only after server readiness)
+The web backend attaches Node `createControlCenterHandler({authenticate, membershipsFor, dashboardRead})` under the paths above. All three functions must be real **server-side** adapters, never stubs for production:
+- `authenticate(req)`: session validation with audience, issuer, CSRF where relevant, expiry, verified subject ID, server verification flag and step-up signal.
+- `membershipsFor(identity)`: database membership for that subject, server-verified roles and active tenant from trusted server-side session; return at most 100 memberships. Never trust query or HTTP role headers.
+- `dashboardRead({identity, tenantId, view, operation})`: data reads under the **same server-side authorization** and tenant row-level policy, not merely a UI check. Return `{status: "VERIFIED", evidenceReceipt, value: 0|integer}` only for measured aggregate counts; otherwise return null.
+
+For Drive, the owner-designated root has already been renamed to `DeviceCare-KI` in Google Drive with metadata readback. Keep the root ID in *private backend configuration* and ensure the service can only list descendants of that root. Historical AI-DeviceCare content is labeled legacy, never automatically promoted to current project evidence. No other project's Drive root is whitelisted.
+
+## Still required before production
+1. Independently verified IAM/OIDC provider, per-tenant membership store, authorized backend host and session hardening. No client-side fake auth.
+2. Durable job lease, per-day Europe/Berlin idempotency, manual STOP/WAIT_OWNER persistence, bounded retries, and trigger execution, separately verified.
+3. Real authorized malware, secret, license, privacy and integrity scanning of file bytes including Google-native conversions. No scan verdict if a provider is missing.


===== "part_06.txt" | file | SHA256 8e3b5f3542cd0df0031daee742c25cfe5e39cee3c1d767311d7f5085dff217aa =====
+4. Archive ZIP + SHA manifest + test restore + legal hold checks + evidence receipts; originals retained.
+5. RBAC/RLS negative and penetration tests, accessibility QA for 320/390 widths, backup/rollback, cost/quota approval and exact production build-identity readback.
+
+### Local test command
+```sh
+node --check web/control-center/iam.mjs
+node --check web/control-center/view.mjs
+node --test tools/drive-governance.test.mjs web/control-center/control-center.test.mjs
+```
+
+### Rollback
+Revert Draft PR #22; no Drive mutations, Android permissions, user accounts, tenant data, identity settings, deployments or production scheduler have been changed by this code.

+function fakeResponse() {
+  const headers={}; let body="";
+  return { headers, statusCode:200,
+    setHeader(k,v){headers[k.toLowerCase()]=v},
+    end(v){body=String(v??"")},
+    get body(){return body},
+  };
+}
+async function run(path,{identityValue=identity(),members=[member(Roles.DEVICE_USER,"personal")],
+    activeTenantId="company-1", data=null, method="GET", auth=true}={}){
+  const response=fakeResponse();
+  const handler=createControlCenterHandler({
+    ...(auth ? {authenticate:async()=>identityValue, membershipsFor:async()=>({memberships:members,activeTenantId})}:{ }),
+    dashboardRead:async()=>data,
+    clock:()=>now,
+  });
+  await handler({method,url:path,headers:{"x-role":"OWNER","x-tenant-id":"company-2"}},response);
+  return response;
+}

+// DeviceCare-KI Control Center: offline, framework-independent authorization core.
+// This module does not authenticate users or provision IAM. It requires server-supplied
+// verified identity + membership; never accept roles or tenant IDs from browser input.
+export const Roles = Object.freeze({
+  OWNER: "OWNER", DEVICE_USER: "DEVICE_USER", B2B_ADMIN: "B2B_ADMIN",
+  ENTERPRISE_ADMIN: "ENTERPRISE_ADMIN", SECURITY_ANALYST: "SECURITY_ANALYST",
+  AUDITOR: "AUDITOR", VIEWER: "VIEWER", FLEET_OPERATOR: "FLEET_OPERATOR",
+});
+const grants = Object.freeze({
+  "personal:view": [Roles.DEVICE_USER, Roles.OWNER, Roles.VIEWER],
+  "tenant:dashboard": [Roles.ENTERPRISE_ADMIN, Roles.B2B_ADMIN, Roles.SECURITY_ANALYST, Roles.AUDITOR, Roles.VIEWER, Roles.FLEET_OPERATOR],
+  "tenant:iam:read": [Roles.ENTERPRISE_ADMIN, Roles.B2B_ADMIN, Roles.AUDITOR],
+  "tenant:iam:manage": [Roles.ENTERPRISE_ADMIN, Roles.B2B_ADMIN],
+  "project:drive:view": [Roles.OWNER, Roles.SECURITY_ANALYST, Roles.AUDITOR],
+  "project:drive:review": [Roles.OWNER, Roles.SECURITY_ANALYST],
+  "project:drive:approve": [Roles.OWNER],
+  "project:drive:pause": [Roles.OWNER],
+  "project:drive:resume": [Roles.OWNER],
+  "project:drive:audit": [Roles.OWNER, Roles.AUDITOR, Roles.SECURITY_ANALYST],
+});
+const stepUp = new Set([
+  "project:drive:approve", "project:drive:pause", "project:drive:resume",
+  "tenant:iam:manage",
+]);
+const MAX_STEPUP_AGE_MS = 5 * 60_000;
+const ROLE_SCOPE = Object.freeze({
+  [Roles.OWNER]: ["project", "personal"],
+  [Roles.DEVICE_USER]: ["personal"],
+  [Roles.ENTERPRISE_ADMIN]: ["tenant"],
+  [Roles.B2B_ADMIN]: ["tenant"],
+  [Roles.SECURITY_ANALYST]: ["tenant", "project"],
+  [Roles.AUDITOR]: ["tenant", "project"],
+  [Roles.VIEWER]: ["tenant", "personal"],
+  [Roles.FLEET_OPERATOR]: ["tenant"],
+});
+const deny = (reason) => ({ allowed: false, reason });
+const allow = () => ({ allowed: true, reason: "VERIFIED" });
+
+export function authorize({ identity, membership, operation, resource, now = new Date() } = {}) {
+  const clock = new Date(now).getTime();
+  if (!Number.isFinite(clock)) return deny("CLOCK_INVALID");
+  if (!identity || identity.authenticated !== true || identity.serverVerified !== true ||
+    typeof identity.subjectId !== "string" || !identity.subjectId ||
+    !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(identity.sessionExpiresAt || "")) ||
+    Date.parse(identity.sessionExpiresAt) <= clock)
+    return deny("AUTH_NOT_VERIFIED");
+  if (!membership || membership.source !== "SERVER" || membership.status !== "VERIFIED" ||
+    membership.active !== true || membership.subjectId !== identity.subjectId ||
+    !Object.values(Roles).includes(membership.role) ||
+    !ROLE_SCOPE[membership.role]?.includes(membership.scope))
+    return deny("MEMBERSHIP_NOT_VERIFIED");
+  if (!resource || !grants[operation]?.includes(membership.role))
+    return deny("PERMISSION_DENIED");
+  const prefix = operation.split(":")[0];
+  if (membership.scope !== prefix || resource.scope !== prefix)
+    return deny("SCOPE_MISMATCH");
+  if (prefix === "personal") {
+    if (!resource.subjectId || resource.subjectId !== identity.subjectId)
+      return deny("PERSONAL_RESOURCE_MISMATCH");
+  } else if (prefix === "tenant") {
+    if (!resource.tenantId || membership.tenantId !== resource.tenantId)
+      return deny("TENANT_ISOLATION");
+    if (resource.crossTenant === true) return deny("TENANT_ISOLATION");
+  } else if (prefix === "project") {
+    if (membership.project !== "DeviceCare-KI" || resource.project !== "DeviceCare-KI")
+      return deny("PROJECT_ISOLATION");
+  } else return deny("UNKNOWN_SCOPE");
+  if (stepUp.has(operation)) {
+    const mfaAt = Date.parse(identity.stepUpAt || "");
+    if (identity.stepUpVerified !== true || !Number.isFinite(mfaAt) ||
+      mfaAt > clock || clock - mfaAt > MAX_STEPUP_AGE_MS)
+      return deny("STEP_UP_REQUIRED");
+  }
+  return allow();
+}
+const targetFor = (operation, { identity, tenantId } = {}) => {
+  if (operation.startsWith("personal:")) return { scope: "personal", subjectId: identity?.subjectId };
+  if (operation.startsWith("tenant:")) return { scope: "tenant", tenantId };
+  return { scope: "project", project: "DeviceCare-KI" };
+};
+export function authorizedMembership({ memberships, identity, operation, tenantId, now } = {}) {
+  if (!Array.isArray(memberships) || memberships.length > 100)
+    return { ...deny("MEMBERSHIPS_UNAVAILABLE"), membership: null };
+  for (const membership of memberships) {
+    const decision = authorize({ identity, membership, operation,
+      resource: targetFor(operation, { identity, tenantId }), now });
+    if (decision.allowed) return { ...decision, membership };
+  }
+  return { ...deny("PERMISSION_DENIED"), membership: null };
+}
+export function tabsFor({ identity, memberships, tenantId, now } = {}) {
+  const choices = [
+    { id: "personal", label: "Mein Profil", operation: "personal:view" },
+    { id: "drive", label: "Drive-Sicherheit", operation: "project:drive:view" },
+    { id: "enterprise", label: "B2B / Enterprise", operation: "tenant:dashboard" },
+    { id: "iam", label: "Zugriffe & IAM", operation: "tenant:iam:read" },
+  ];
+  return choices.filter(c=>authorizedMembership({
+    identity, memberships, operation: c.operation, tenantId, now
+  }).allowed).map(({ id, label })=>({ id,label }));
+}
+export function validateOwnerPlanApproval({ identity, memberships, approval, plan, now = new Date() } = {}) {
+  const projectDecision = authorizedMembership({
+    identity, memberships, operation:"project:drive:approve", now
+  });
+  if (!projectDecision.allowed) return deny(projectDecision.reason);
+  if (!approval || !plan || approval.actorId !== identity.subjectId ||
+    approval.project !== "DeviceCare-KI" || plan.project !== "DeviceCare-KI" ||
+    approval.status !== "VERIFIED" || !approval.serverVerified ||
+    !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(approval.planSha256 || "") ||
+    approval.planSha256 !== plan.sha256 ||
+    approval.resourceVersion !== plan.resourceVersion ||
+    plan.currentReadbackVerified !== true || plan.securityGatesVerified !== true ||
+    !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(approval.expiresAt || "")) ||
+    Date.parse(approval.expiresAt) <= new Date(now).getTime())
+    return deny("APPROVAL_STALE_OR_INVALID");
+  // This is a validation decision only, never a Drive or IAM mutation.
+  return allow();
+}
+
+const TENANT_DELEGATABLE_ROLES = new Set([
+  Roles.VIEWER, Roles.FLEET_OPERATOR, Roles.AUDITOR, Roles.SECURITY_ANALYST,
+]);
+// A tenant administrator cannot promote themself or grant administrative,
+// project-owner, service-agent, or cross-tenant privileges.
+export function evaluateTenantRoleChange({ identity, membership, change,
+  now = new Date() } = {}) {
+  const scope = { scope:"tenant", tenantId:change?.tenantId };
+  const decision = authorize({ identity, membership,
+    operation:"tenant:iam:manage", resource:scope, now });
+  if (!decision.allowed) return decision;
+  if (!change || change.status!=="VERIFIED" || !change.serverVerified ||
+    !change.actorIntentVerified || !change.targetSubjectId ||
+    change.targetSubjectId===identity.subjectId ||
+    !TENANT_DELEGATABLE_ROLES.has(change.newRole) ||
+    change.expectedMembershipVersion===undefined ||
+    !change.targetMembershipVerified ||
+    change.lastAdminProtected!==true)
+    return deny("TENANT_ROLE_CHANGE_NOT_APPROVED");
+  // No mutation or IAM provider call. Actual write must independently recheck
+  // target membership, tenant version and step-up immediately before mutation.
+  return { allowed:true, reason:"PLAN_ONLY" };

+import { authorizedMembership, tabsFor } from "./iam.mjs";
+
+// SSR-only private adapter. No OAuth provider, scan worker, database or server startup
+// is included. These must be injected after independent verification.
+const paths = Object.freeze({
+  "/profile/control-center": { id: "personal", operation: "personal:view", heading: "Mein Geräteprofil" },
+  "/profile/control-center/drive": { id: "drive", operation: "project:drive:view", heading: "Drive-Sicherheit" },
+  "/profile/control-center/enterprise": { id: "enterprise", operation: "tenant:dashboard", heading: "Enterprise-Übersicht" },
+  "/profile/control-center/iam": { id: "iam", operation: "tenant:iam:read", heading: "Identitäten & Rollen" },
+});
+const h = value => String(value ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g,
+  symbol => ({ "&":"&amp;", "<":"&lt;", ">":"&gt;", '"':"&quot;", "'":"&#39;" })[symbol]);
+function displayProof(data) {
+  if (!data || data.status !== "VERIFIED" || !data.evidenceReceipt)
+    return { status: "NOT_PROVEN", label: "Noch keine geprüften Daten vorhanden", value: "–" };
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(data.value) || data.value < 0)
+    return { status: "NOT_PROVEN", label: "Messwert nicht verifiziert", value: "–" };
+  return { status: "VERIFIED", label: "Nachweis vorhanden", value: String(data.value) };
+}
+function document({ title, tabId, tabs, metric, status }) {
+  const nav = tabs.map(tab=> {
+    const href = tab.id==="personal" ? "/profile/control-center" : "/profile/control-center/"+tab.id;
+    return `<a href="${h(href)}" ${tab.id===tabId ? 'aria-current="page"' : ""}>${h(tab.label)}</a>`;
+  }).join("");
+  return `<!doctype html>

+const baseHeaders = {
+  "Cache-Control": "no-store, private",
+  "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
+  "X-Frame-Options": "DENY",
+  "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer",
+  "Content-Security-Policy": "default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; base-uri 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'; form-action 'none'",
+};
+export function createControlCenterHandler({ authenticate, membershipsFor, dashboardRead, clock = () => new Date() } = {}) {
+  // These adapters MUST be server-side and cannot rely on caller-supplied role headers.
+  return async function handler(req, res) {
+    for (const [k,v] of Object.entries(baseHeaders)) res.setHeader(k,v);
+    const fail = (status, msg) => { res.statusCode=status; res.setHeader("Content-Type","text/plain; charset=utf-8");res.end(msg); };
+    if (req.method !== "GET") return fail(405,"METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED");
+    const path = req.url?.split("?")[0];
+    const route = Object.hasOwn(paths, path) ? paths[path] : null;
+    if (!route) return fail(404,"NOT_FOUND");
+    if (typeof authenticate !== "function" || typeof membershipsFor !== "function")
+      return fail(503,"IAM_NOT_CONFIGURED");
+    try {
+      const identity = await authenticate(req); // must be verified server-side
+      const context = await membershipsFor(identity); // server-side memberships, tenant ID from trusted session
+      const memberships = context?.memberships;
+      const tenantId = context?.activeTenantId;
+      const now = clock();
+      const decision = authorizedMembership({ identity, memberships,
+        operation:route.operation, tenantId, now });
+      if (!decision.allowed) return fail(403,"FORBIDDEN");
+      const tabs = tabsFor({ identity, memberships, tenantId, now });
+      const evidence = typeof dashboardRead==="function"
+        ? await dashboardRead({ identity, tenantId, view: route.id, operation:route.operation })
+        : null;
+      const metric = displayProof(evidence);
+      res.statusCode = 200;
+      res.setHeader("Content-Type","text/html; charset=utf-8");
+      res.end(document({ title:route.heading, tabId:route.id, tabs, metric, status:metric.status }));
+    } catch {
+      return fail(503,"SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"); // never leak errors or authentication internals
+    }
+  };
+}

+# Play Integrity für DeviceCare-KI
+
+Firebase App Check verwendet in einem gezielt konfigurierten Release-Build
+den Play-Integrity-Anbieter für `com.aistudio.formwerk.vktmrx`. Er wird beim
+App-Start vor der Oberfläche registriert. Firebase bewertet die Attestierung
+bei Firebase-Anfragen. Die lokale Geräteinspektion verwendet weiterhin ihre
+eigenen Datenquellen. App Check liefert der App kein separates Geräteurteil
+für den Inspektionsbericht.
+
+## Interner Test
+
+1. In der Play Console unter App-Integrität das zur App gehörende Firebase-
+   bzw. Google-Cloud-Projekt mit der Play Integrity API verknüpfen.
+2. In Firebase unter App Check den Play-Integrity-Anbieter für dasselbe
+   Android-App-Objekt registrieren. Für den Play-Testtrack den SHA-256-
+   Fingerabdruck des **Play-App-Signing-Zertifikats** verwenden. Die
+   projektbezogene `google-services.json` lokal bzw. im freigegebenen
+   Build-System bereitstellen. Die effektive Enforcement-Konfiguration
+   für die genutzten Firebase-Produkte prüfen.
+3. Mit dem bestehenden Upload-Schlüssel und einer im Testtrack zulässigen
+   Versionsnummer bauen. Die Signaturkonfiguration des Projekts verwenden.
+
+   ```sh
+   gradle --no-daemon -Pdevicecare.playIntegrity=true :app:bundleRelease
+   ```
+
+4. Das Bundle nach Freigabe im internen Play-Testtrack bereitstellen. Auf
+   dem ZTE Blade V70 mit dem Testkonto über Google Play installieren. Einen
+   normalen Firebase-Aufruf ausführen und im Firebase-Projekt die
+   App-Check-Anfragen prüfen. Den Ergebnisstatus, Build und Installationsweg
+   dokumentieren. Zusätzlich die lokale Inspektion im Flugmodus ausführen.
+
+Debug-Builds verwenden Firebase Debug App Check. Ein Debug-Build und die
+Integritätsprüfung in der Play-Store-App liefern keinen Nachweis für die
+App-Check-Integration des DeviceCare-Release-Builds.
+
+## Rückweg
+
+Ohne `-Pdevicecare.playIntegrity=true` verwendet ein Release-Build den
+bisherigen reCAPTCHA-Anbieter. Bei einem Rückbau nach einer Provider-
+Umstellung im Firebase-Projekt zuerst prüfen, welcher Anbieter dort
+registriert ist und unter der aktuellen Richtlinie akzeptiert wird.
+
+Quellen
+
+- [Firebase App Check mit Play Integrity](https://firebase.google.com/docs/app-check/android/play-integrity-provider)
+- [Interne Tests in Google Play](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/9845334?hl=de)

TITLE: feat(TRUST): bounded offline JVM / Go stack-dump import contract
BASE: main <- HEAD: feature/offline-stack-dump-contract-p0 (3f16621d976c94494589ca24b5c72b3dd3168f53)
BODY: ## Scope
Offline user-supplied UTF-8 InputStream parser under existing :app; no new dependencies or permissions. Parses JVM states, generic Thread-N headers, Go IDs/states and known network-stack frames. IDs are never treated as live goroutine counts or packet/connection counts.

## Security / privacy
4 MiB / 100k lines / 16k chars-per-line limits; reject malformed UTF-8 and NUL; fixed error codes; no raw dump persistence, no name/endpoint leak, no background collection, no cloud uploads. No changes to Android manifest, Firebase, inspection UI, signing or scoring.

- QUALITY: synthetic positive/negative tests include JVM states, Go ID-vs-count, network stack markers, truncation, PII redaction and rejection of bad input.

--- /dev/null
+++ b/app/src/main/java/com/example/core/stackdump/OfflineStackDumpAnalyzer.kt
@@ -0,0 +1,160 @@
+package com.example.core.stackdump
+
+import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
+import java.io.InputStream
+import java.nio.ByteBuffer
+import java.nio.charset.CharacterCodingException
+import java.nio.charset.CodingErrorAction
+import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets
+
+/** Offline import of an explicitly supplied text stream; never inspects another process. */
+object StackDumpContract {
+  const val VERSION = "devicecare-stackdump/1"
+  const val MAX_IMPORT_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024
+  const val MAX_LINES = 100_000
+  const val MAX_LINE_CHARS = 16_384
+  const val MAX_REPORTED_GO_IDS = 64
+}
+
+enum class StackEvidenceStatus { VERIFIED, SUSPECTED, NOT_PROVEN }
+
+data class StackEvidence(
+  val code: String,
+  val status: StackEvidenceStatus,
+  val occurrences: Int,
+)
+
+data class StackDumpAssessment(
+  val contractVersion: String = StackDumpContract.VERSION,
+  /** Captured JVM headers are not a complete OS thread census. */
+  val jvmHeaders: Int,
+  val jvmStates: Map<String, Int>,
+  val genericJvmThreadHeaders: Int,
+  /** Parsed Go headers; NOT an active goroutine count. */
+  val goHeaders: Int,
+  val goIdsSample: List<Long>,
+  val goIdsSampleTruncated: Boolean,
+  val goStates: Map<String, Int>,
+  /** Stack frame appearances, NOT flows, packets or unique connections. */
+  val networkFrameOccurrences: Map<String, Int>,
+  val evidence: List<StackEvidence>,
+) {
+  val liveGoroutineCount: Int? = null
+}
+
+/**
+ * Caller owns/closes input. No persistence, networking or Android APIs.
+ * Fixed error codes avoid leaking source data through validation errors.
+ */
+object OfflineStackDumpAnalyzer {
+  private val jvmHeader =
+    Regex("""^Thread:\s+(.+?)\s+\[id=[0-9]+\s+state=([A-Z_]+)\b""")
+  private val genericThread = Regex("""Thread-[0-9]+""")
+  private val goHeader = Regex("""^goroutine\s+([0-9]+)\s+\[([^\]]+)\]:""")
+
+  fun import(input: InputStream): StackDumpAssessment {
+    val data = ByteArrayOutputStream()
+    val buffer = ByteArray(8192)
+    var emptyReads = 0
+    while (true) {
+      val count = input.read(buffer)
+      if (count == -1) break
+      if (count == 0) {
+        require(++emptyReads <= 3) { "STACK_IMPORT_STALLED" }
+        continue
+      }
+      emptyReads = 0
+      require(data.size() <= StackDumpContract.MAX_IMPORT_BYTES - count) { "STACK_IMPORT_TOO_LARGE" }
+      data.write(buffer, 0, count)
+    }
+    val content =
+      try {
+        StandardCharsets.UTF_8.newDecoder()
+          .onMalformedInput(CodingErrorAction.REPORT)
+          .onUnmappableCharacter(CodingErrorAction.REPORT)
+          .decode(ByteBuffer.wrap(data.toByteArray())).toString()
+      } catch (_: CharacterCodingException) {
+        throw IllegalArgumentException("STACK_IMPORT_INVALID_UTF8")
+      }
+    require('\u0000' !in content) { "STACK_IMPORT_BINARY_CONTENT" }
+
+    var section = ""
+    var lines = 0
+    var jvmCount = 0
+    var genericCount = 0
+    var goCount = 0
+    val jvmStates = linkedMapOf<String, Int>()
+    val goStates = linkedMapOf<String, Int>()
+    val goIds = ArrayList<Long>()
+    val frames = linkedMapOf<String, Int>()
+    content.lineSequence().forEach { line ->
+      lines++
+      require(lines <= StackDumpContract.MAX_LINES) { "STACK_IMPORT_TOO_MANY_LINES" }
+      require(line.length <= StackDumpContract.MAX_LINE_CHARS) { "STACK_IMPORT_LINE_TOO_LONG" }
+      when (line.trim()) {
+        "=== JVM STACK ===" -> section = "JVM"
+        "=== GO STACK ===" -> section = "GO"
+      }
+      if (section == "JVM") {
+        val match = jvmHeader.find(line)
+        if (match != null) {
+          jvmCount++
+          if (genericThread.matches(match.groupValues[1])) genericCount++
+          val state = match.groupValues[2].takeIf {
+            it in setOf("RUNNABLE", "WAITING", "TIMED_WAITING", "BLOCKED", "NEW", "TERMINATED")
+          } ?: "OTHER"
+          jvmStates[state] = (jvmStates[state] ?: 0) + 1
+        }
+      }
+      if (section == "GO") {
+        val match = goHeader.find(line)
+        if (match != null) {
+          goCount++
+          val id = match.groupValues[1].toLongOrNull()
+          if (id != null && goIds.size < StackDumpContract.MAX_REPORTED_GO_IDS) goIds.add(id)
+          val state = match.groupValues[2].substringBefore(',').trim().let {
+            when (it) {
+              "running", "runnable", "select", "chan receive", "IO wait",
+              "syscall", "sync.WaitGroup.Wait", "sleep" -> it
+              else -> "other"
+            }
+          }
+          goStates[state] = (goStates[state] ?: 0) + 1
+        }
+      }
+      if (section.isNotEmpty()) {
+        val category = when {
+          "intra/dnsx/" in line -> "DNS"
+          "netstack/udp.go" in line || "gonet.(*UDPConn)" in line -> "UDP"
+          "netstack/tcp.go" in line || "gonet.(*TCPConn)" in line -> "TCP"
+          "tun2socks" in line || "tunnel/tunnel.go" in line -> "VPN_TUN"
+          "SocketInputStream.socketRead" in line -> "SOCKET_IO"
+          else -> null
+        }
+        if (category != null) frames[category] = (frames[category] ?: 0) + 1
+      }
+    }
+    val evidence = mutableListOf<StackEvidence>()
+    if (jvmCount > 0) evidence.add(StackEvidence("JVM_HEADERS_PARSED", StackEvidenceStatus.VERIFIED, jvmCount))
+    if (goCount > 0) evidence.add(StackEvidence("GO_HEADERS_PARSED_NOT_LIVE_COUNT", StackEvidenceStatus.VERIFIED, goCount))
+    if (genericCount > 0) evidence.add(StackEvidence("GENERIC_JVM_THREAD_HEADERS", StackEvidenceStatus.VERIFIED, genericCount))
+    if (frames.isNotEmpty()) evidence.add(StackEvidence("NETWORK_FRAMES_OBSERVED", StackEvidenceStatus.VERIFIED, frames.values.sum()))
+    if (genericCount >= 64) evidence.add(StackEvidence("HIGH_GENERIC_THREAD_HEADER_COUNT", StackEvidenceStatus.SUSPECTED, genericCount))
+    if (jvmCount == 0 && goCount == 0) {
+      evidence.add(StackEvidence("NO_RECOGNIZED_HEADERS", StackEvidenceStatus.NOT_PROVEN, 0))
+    }
+    evidence.add(StackEvidence("RESOURCE_LEAK_NOT_ESTABLISHED", StackEvidenceStatus.NOT_PROVEN, 0))
+    evidence.add(StackEvidence("MALWARE_NOT_ESTABLISHED", StackEvidenceStatus.NOT_PROVEN, 0))
+    return StackDumpAssessment(
+      jvmHeaders = jvmCount,
+      jvmStates = jvmStates.toMap(),
+      genericJvmThreadHeaders = genericCount,
+      goHeaders = goCount,
+      goIdsSample = goIds.toList(),
+      goIdsSampleTruncated = goCount > goIds.size,
+      goStates = goStates.toMap(),
+      networkFrameOccurrences = frames.toMap(),
+      evidence = evidence.toList(),
+    )
+  }
+}

+package com.example.core.stackdump
+
+import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
+import org.junit.Assert.assertThrows
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+class OfflineStackDumpAnalyzerTest {
+  private fun parse(text: String) =
+    OfflineStackDumpAnalyzer.import(ByteArrayInputStream(text.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)))
+
+  @Test fun jvmStatesAndGenericNamesAreCountedWithoutExposingNames() {
+    val report = parse(
+      """
+      === JVM STACK ===
+      Thread: Thread-356  [id=580  state=RUNNABLE  daemon=false]
+      Thread: main  [id=2  state=WAITING  daemon=false]
+      Thread: Thread-7  [id=11  state=TIMED_WAITING  daemon=false]
+      Thread: user@example.com  [id=12  state=BLOCKED  daemon=false]
+      """.trimIndent()
+    )
+    assertEquals(4, report.jvmHeaders)
+    assertEquals(2, report.genericJvmThreadHeaders)
+    assertEquals(1, report.jvmStates["RUNNABLE"])
+    assertEquals(1, report.jvmStates["WAITING"])
+    assertEquals(1, report.jvmStates["TIMED_WAITING"])
+    assertEquals(1, report.jvmStates["BLOCKED"])
+    assertFalse(report.toString().contains("user@example.com"))
+  }
+
+  @Test fun goIdsAreNotLiveCounts() {
+    val report = parse(
+      """
+      === GO STACK ===
+      goroutine 189506 [select, 1 minutes]:
+      goroutine 172979 [IO wait, 8 minutes]:
+      goroutine 84 [running, locked to thread]:
+      """.trimIndent()
+    )
+    assertEquals(3, report.goHeaders)
+    assertEquals(listOf(189506L, 172979L, 84L), report.goIdsSample)
+    assertEquals(1, report.goStates["IO wait"])
+    assertNull(report.liveGoroutineCount)
+    assertTrue(report.evidence.any { it.code == "RESOURCE_LEAK_NOT_ESTABLISHED" && it.status == StackEvidenceStatus.NOT_PROVEN })
+  }
+
+  @Test fun networkFrameAppearancesAreNotConnectionCounts() {
+    val report = parse(
+      """
+      === JVM STACK ===
+      at java.net.SocketInputStream.socketRead0(Native Method)
+      === GO STACK ===
+      github.com/celzero/firestack/intra/dnsx/transport.go:1136
+      github.com/celzero/firestack/intra/netstack/udp.go:336
+      github.com/celzero/firestack/intra/netstack/tcp.go:336
+      github.com/celzero/firestack/intra/tun2socks.go:446
+      """.trimIndent()
+    )
+    assertEquals(1, report.networkFrameOccurrences["DNS"])
+    assertEquals(1, report.networkFrameOccurrences["UDP"])
+    assertEquals(1, report.networkFrameOccurrences["TCP"])
+    assertEquals(1, report.networkFrameOccurrences["VPN_TUN"])
+    assertEquals(1, report.networkFrameOccurrences["SOCKET_IO"])
+    assertEquals(0, report.goHeaders)
+  }
+
+  @Test fun manyHeadersAreSuspectedButNotProvenLeaks() {
+    val data = buildString {
+      appendLine("=== JVM STACK ===")
+      repeat(64) { appendLine("Thread: Thread-$it [id=$it state=RUNNABLE daemon=false]") }
+    }
+    val report = parse(data)
+    assertTrue(report.evidence.any { it.code == "HIGH_GENERIC_THREAD_HEADER_COUNT" && it.status == StackEvidenceStatus.SUSPECTED })
+    assertTrue(report.evidence.any { it.code == "RESOURCE_LEAK_NOT_ESTABLISHED" && it.status == StackEvidenceStatus.NOT_PROVEN })
+  }
+
+  @Test fun sampledIdsHaveHardCapWithoutInflatingLiveCounts() {
+    val data = buildString {
+      appendLine("=== GO STACK ===")
+      repeat(80) { appendLine("goroutine " + (170000 + it) + " [select]:") }
+    }
+    val report = parse(data)
+    assertEquals(80, report.goHeaders)
+    assertEquals(64, report.goIdsSample.size)
+    assertTrue(report.goIdsSampleTruncated)
+    assertNull(report.liveGoroutineCount)
+  }
+
+  @Test fun rejectsOversizeInvalidUtf8BinaryAndLongLine() {
+    assertEquals("STACK_IMPORT_TOO_LARGE",
+      assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
+        OfflineStackDumpAnalyzer.import(ByteArrayInputStream(ByteArray(StackDumpContract.MAX_IMPORT_BYTES + 1)))
+      }.message)
+    assertEquals("STACK_IMPORT_INVALID_UTF8",
+      assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
+        OfflineStackDumpAnalyzer.import(ByteArrayInputStream(byteArrayOf(0xC3.toByte(), 0x28)))
+      }.message)
+    assertEquals("STACK_IMPORT_BINARY_CONTENT",
+      assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) { parse("=== JVM STACK ===\u0000") }.message)
+    assertEquals("STACK_IMPORT_LINE_TOO_LONG",
+      assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException::class.java) {
+        parse("=== GO STACK ===\n" + "z".repeat(StackDumpContract.MAX_LINE_CHARS + 1))
+      }.message)
+  }
+
+  @Test fun unmarkedTextFailsClosedAndNeverPretendsMalwareProof() {
+    val report = parse("Thread: Thread-1 [id=1 state=RUNNABLE]\ngoroutine 123 [running]:")
+    assertEquals(0, report.jvmHeaders)
+    assertEquals(0, report.goHeaders)
+    assertTrue(report.evidence.any { it.code == "NO_RECOGNIZED_HEADERS" && it.status == StackEvidenceStatus.NOT_PROVEN })
+    assertTrue(report.evidence.any { it.code == "MALWARE_NOT_ESTABLISHED" && it.status == StackEvidenceStatus.NOT_PROVEN })
+    assertEquals(StackDumpContract.VERSION, report.contractVersion)
+  }
+}

+# STACK-DUMP-IMPORT@1 — offline diagnostic contract
+
+Entry point: OfflineStackDumpAnalyzer.import(input: InputStream), in existing :app module.
+
+- Explicit user-provided UTF-8 text only. No process inspection, root, ADB, Android permissions, AccessibilityService, package enumeration, networking, remote inference, background capture, or file persistence.
+- Caller opens and closes the stream. A future user-facing file picker can use Android ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT; this code-only contract does not include a file-picker UI.
+- Limits: 4 MiB, 100,000 lines, 16,384 characters per line. Invalid UTF-8/NUL and oversize input fail closed with constant codes.
+- Aggregates only: recognized JVM header states, generic Thread-N counts, captured Go header states, bounded (64) Go identifiers, and occurrences of known network stack frames. Raw names, domains, endpoints, and stack text are never returned.
+- Go identifiers are IDs, not counts. Go header count is NOT a census of active goroutines; network stack-frame occurrences are NOT packet or connection counts.
+- VERIFIED means observed in user-supplied text. SUSPECTED flags a diagnostic hypothesis only (64+ generic JVM headers). NOT_PROVEN rules out automatic claims of resource leaks or malware.
+- No automatic scoring upgrade; no modifications to existing Android observation contracts.
+- Synthetic positive/negative JUnit tests cover headers, IDs, redaction, network markers, caps and malformed input.
+- Gate: existing android-ci.yml runs unit tests, Android lint, Trivy vulnerability/secret/license/misconfig scanning, debug APK signing and SHA evidence. Physical Android 15 acceptance stays NOT_PROVEN until performed.
+- Rollback: revert this standalone commit without affecting user data, Android permissions, Firebase, or existing QA screens.
+- Independent review lenses: PRODUCT (contract), TRUST (privacy/budgets), QUALITY (tests/CI).

+---
+name: trust-android-malware-diagnosis
+description: Evidence-bound, privacy-preserving assessment of suspicious Android apps from pre-validated data. Use only in explicit local diagnosis or scoped synthetic validation.
+version: 1.0.0
+domain: TRUST
+---
+
+# Android Malware Diagnosis Skill
+
+Follow the repository root AGENTS.md. This skill grants no Android, Firebase, network, signing, or release permissions.
+
+## Role
+You interpret only supplied, provenance-checked, version-matched app metadata and code/runtime facts. Never claim to have performed a live scan from chat or that you can read privileged data from an ordinary Android app.
+
+## Deterministic evaluation
+- Generate at most 3 explicit candidate hypotheses from matching static indicators and relevant declared/granted capabilities. A large permission set, obfuscation, sideload or reflection is not proof.
+- Evaluate each against real direct evidence and concrete counterevidence. Valid statuses: BESTÄTIGT, TEILWEISE, WIDERLEGT, OFFEN. Absence and conflict are OFFEN, not WIDERLEGT.
+- Consider the actual app purpose and the user's explicit expectations. Unknown purpose blocks HOCH classification. Do not accuse legitimate messenger/backup workflows by default.
+- Apply policy from LocalMalwareDiagnosticEngine rather than letting an LLM invent a severity. HOCH requires direct linked proof; uncertain but potentially severe claims are MITTEL with beobachten.
+- Return one JSON object with a verdict field containing package, risk, main_reason, hypotheses, confidence, false_positive_risk, recommendation, user_explanation; show a short plain-language explanation outside JSON when requested.
+- Confidence is evidence coverage, never a validated malware probability.
+
+## Privacy and platform constraints
+- NEVER read, log, transmit or present raw SMS contents, personal contact entries, packet payloads, endpoint/IP lists, tokens or accessibility text.
+- No root, ADB, hidden APIs, AccessibilityService automation, covert monitoring, background scan, forced removal or permission mutation.
+- Only explicit user-started inspections. Missing Android third-party telemetry must be represented as missing.
+- Do not send security evidence to Gemini/Firebase/other cloud providers automatically.
+- Unverified user input and LLM claims never qualify as VERIFIED_RUNTIME_TRACE or VERIFIED_STATIC_REVIEW.
+
+## Self-check before publishing
+1. Was counterevidence inspected for each candidate and was its absence recorded accurately?
+2. Does each HOCH/KRITISCH decision have version-bound direct proof?
+3. Could a legitimate power user or messenger case be misclassified?
+4. Can an end user understand the explanation, and is the output free of raw sensitive data?
+5. Are unit/lint/build/security/real-device gates recorded with real evidence instead of assumed green?
+
+Further normative technical details: docs/security/LIVE-MALWARE-DIAGNOSIS-V1.md.
