# AI-DeviceCare — Product & Engineering Masterplan (Android-first)

**Status:** M1 BASE AND ADAPTIVE PROFILE MERGED / ADAPTIVE DEVICE QA OPEN / NO PRODUCTION RELEASE
**Historical initial repository audit (2026-10-31):** public `main`; `README.md` = `# AI-DeviceCare`; no `AGENTS.md`, Android Gradle project, AndroidManifest or existing issues found.
**Product positioning:** privacy-first Android Security & Device Optimization, **no root / no ADB required**; Google Play commercial distribution; iOS separate feature-reduced concept. **Two intended commercial products:** `AI-DeviceCare` (consumer) and `AI-DeviceCare+` (business/enterprise), with clearly differentiated scope and app IDs to avoid Play repetitive-content policy issues. **Milestone-first launch:** ship useful, fully tested Free/Starter functionality before expensive AV/network/enterprise modules.

**NOT_PROVEN.** For #7, physical coverage of adaptive sampling, OEM behavior, TalkBack speech and CPU, battery and thermal measurements remain open. The repeat ZTE test is out of scope at the owner's request. Signed-release reproducibility, Play and billing acceptance, and production readiness remain open.

## 1. Non-negotiable functional contract
- Do **not** promise bulk deletion of all private caches or direct arbitrary changes to other apps' system permissions.
- Android public APIs, user-facing settings intents and approved storage APIs only.
- Every security finding must contain **evidence source, timestamp, confidence, impact, recommended action, reversibility**, and known blind spots. No ungrounded spyware claims.
- Deterministic safe remediation + explicit user authorization; no autonomous high-risk settings changes, no silent app uninstall or erasure.
- Zero sensitive data collection by default, on-device first, consent-gated opt-in cloud scans; never upload whole APKs or user files without explicit per-action consent.
- Device diagnostics and logging must not claim kernel access; Android `BatteryManager` exposes *device-dependent* battery properties (level, charging, temperature, cycle count, current when supported); direct per-app battery power use is not generally available. `UsageStatsManager` requires a user-enabled special access and measures usage rather than exact mAh.
- Do not over-sample sensors or run persistent foreground work to power an "optimizer" that itself drains the battery. Prefer event-driven sampling, WorkManager and configurable limits.
- Repair policy: never pretend to "repair hardware"; support guided steps, safe operations, explicit consent, validation and transparency about unknown/unavailable signals.
- Observability: opt-in remote diagnostics, local-only default, retention TTL, size cap, redaction (no passwords, messages, raw browsing history, VPN traffic payloads), encrypted export, user deletion and access audit.
- App-store approval is **not guaranteed**; Play policy gate before sensitive permissions or SDK selection.
- `QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES` only if antivirus core requires it and Google Play grants declaration; prefer scoped queries.
- `MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` only if approved, justified core AV/file-management function; `StorageManager.ACTION_CLEAR_APP_CACHE` requires this permission and user consent; this is not a universal internal-cache wipe.
- Avoid AccessibilityService to automate settings clicks/permissions; Google Play restricts autonomous Accessibility use, and cleaners are not accessibility tools.
- One `VpnService` per user/profile; local DNS filtering/firewall vs remote WireGuard tunnel modes must be integrated, not concurrently independent VPNs.
- No claims of zero-day / ransomware completeness; machine learning is supplementary to verified signature/rules and explainable indicators.

## 2. Product domains / epics
| ID | Module | Implementation | Tier |
|---|---|---|---|
| CORE | Onboarding + dashboard + threat model + transparent findings | Kotlin, Jetpack Compose, Room, WorkManager, DataStore | Free |
| INV | Installed-app inventory + app metadata/declared and grant state where exposed; system apps | PackageManager, permission APIs, scoped visibility | Free |
| PERM | Risk engine for permissions, device admin, accessibility, notification access, VPN config, unknown sources, overlays, battery and patch posture where readable | Evidence collectors + risk rules, permissions and settings intents | Free |
| ACT | Safe remediation orchestration: one-tap **where permitted**, otherwise deep-link + guided user confirmation | Capability matrix / action lifecycle / audit log / rollback if possible | Free/Pro |
| CLEAN | Own cache, files chosen by user, duplicates and large files, StorageManager storage-management screens; explicit preview | SAF / MediaStore / StorageManager; don't erase app data | Free/Pro |
| AV | Authorized APK/file metadata and file-scanning, signed YARA rules, hash reputation, static analysis, optional ML triage and cloud reputation | Signatures + YARA, AV test vectors; score not sole verdict | Pro |
| NET | DNS-based ad/tracker/malware domain filtering with allow/deny lists and leak-safe handling | one local VPN service, encrypted DNS resolver; per-app policy if supported | Pro |
| VPN | optional paid remote VPN via WireGuard-compatible provider with contractual rights; DNS/firewall integrated in same tunnel stack | VPNService, egress infrastructure, kill-switch behavior tests | Plus optional |
| DEV | Device posture: patch level, storage, battery estimates, network changes, integrity where authorized | Public OS APIs / optional Play Integrity where eligible | Free/Pro |
| BAT | Battery Intelligence: level, charging, temperature, health indicator, cycle count/current if supported; temporal trends and guided battery optimization | BatteryManager, ACTION_BATTERY_CHANGED; optional UsageStatsManager with user-granted usage access; no false app-level energy attribution | Free/Starter/Pro |
| DIAG | Device diagnostics and remediation: symptoms, correlations, repair suggestions, permissioned settings intents, rechecks, safe rollback | Diagnostics collectors + action planner + Android intents | Starter/Pro |
| LOG | Privacy-preserving local diagnostics and event logging, optional consent-gated support export, retention and PII redaction | Room, structured logs, ring buffer, encrypted export, retention policy | Free/Starter/Pro |
| FLEET | Business/Enterprise: organization enrollment, SSO/RBAC, aggregated posture, device fleet reporting and optional approved MDM policies | Separate app experience, authorized Android Enterprise enrollment / DevicePolicyManager only when eligible, backend tenant isolation | Enterprise/Business |
| AI | Explainable recommendations and prioritization based on rule-based evidence; opt-in local ML; cloud LLM for explanation only, never direct auto-execution | local rules + confidence + redaction + consent | Pro |
| BILL | Play Billing, subscriptions / entitlement verification, privacy transparency | Google Play Billing + backend entitlements | Pro |
| AUDIT | encrypted local reports, one-click export with redaction, provenance of remediation | Room/SQLCipher if license/security reviewed + JSON/PDF export | Pro |

## 3. Architecture
```mermaid
flowchart TD
 U[Android user / Jetpack Compose] --> A[Consent + Settings Guidance + Dashboard]
 A --> C[Android Capability & Permission Gate]
 C --> I[App Metadata/Permission Collector]
 C --> D[Device/Posture Collector]
 C --> F[User-Selected File Collector]
 C --> N[VPNService DNS/Firewall Collector]
 I --> E[Local Evidence Store]
 D --> E
 F --> E
 N --> E
 E --> R[Deterministic Policy & Risk Scoring]
 R --> M[Local AI / optional opt-in cloud explanation]
 R --> X[Remediation Planner]
 X --> S{Action allowed?}
 S -->|Public API / reversible| P[Explicitly confirm -> Execute -> Verify]
 S -->|System-controlled| H[Open Android settings -> Guide -> Re-read]
 S -->|Not exposed| Z[Explain limitation]
 E --> O[Transparent report / export]
 ```

## 4. Delivery roadmap and acceptance gates
### P0 / 1-2 weeks — Repository foundation & compliance
- Create `AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, `docs/PRODUCT_REQUIREMENTS.md`, `docs/ANDROID_CAPABILITY_MATRIX.md`, `docs/PLAY_POLICY_MATRIX.md`, `docs/THREAT_MODEL.md`, `docs/LICENSE_AUDIT.md`.
- Kotlin + Compose modular scaffold, build reproducibility, Gradle dependency locking/verification, Dependabot, Android lint, unit tests, CodeQL where supported, secret scanning, dependency/license audit, release signing plan.
- Definition of Done: clean debug AAB/APK build and CI on Android 15 emulator; no restricted permissions by default.

### P1 / 2-4 weeks — MVP audit & explainable recommendations
- App inventory; app/system settings shortcuts; permissions where exposed; posture; **battery health and charging dashboards, usage-derived hints where authorized, minimal local structured diagnostics/logging**, risk scoring; evidence and false-positive UX.
- Offline policy pack, no opaque "AI proof" claims.
- DoD: test matrix across Android 12-16 devices; system settings tested; no data deletion.

### P2 / 2-3 weeks — Cleaner and remediation
- Storage analytics; own-cache cleanup; user-authorized file cleanup; per-app settings handoff, verification, undo where possible; test guard against accidental data loss.
- DoD: before/after byte evidence; consent + dry run + validation; zero silent destructive actions.

### P3 / 3-5 weeks — Antimalware
- Hash/signature + YARA/static rule pack + suspicious behavior **indicators only** (not kernel or inaccessible runtime monitoring); ML triage with calibration, false-positive tracking and opt-in cloud reputation.
- DoD: real labeled corpus incl. clean benign apps and available ransomware samples in isolated environment; sensitivity, specificity, FPR and FNR reported; unknowns shown as unknown.

### P4 / 3-5 weeks — DNS, firewall, VPN
- One VPNService-based networking engine; DNS malware/ad/tracker filtering; optional encrypted DNS; explicit VPN conflict warnings; optional remote VPN integration after provider license/cost tests.
- DoD: DNS leak tests, IPv4/IPv6, connectivity under captive portal, Always-on, battery and throughput tests, VPN policy declaration.

### P5 / 2-3 weeks — Monetization and Store
- Play Billing subscription, entitlements and offline grace period, data safety disclosures, privacy policy, permissions declarations, optional VPN demo evidence; accessibility/usability and German/English storefront.
- DoD: internal track -> closed testing -> staged production only after policy approval + security evidence + user opt-in.

## 5. Licensing shortlist (review before inclusion)
- YARA: BSD-3-Clause (rule content has separate rights); potential match for local static scanning.
- WireGuard tunnel: assess each included Android/library/dependency license, provider entitlement and VPN service costs before integration.
- ClamAV: GPLv2; **do not directly embed in proprietary mobile app** without compatible licensing/legal review; possible distinct backend deployment requires analysis.
- AdGuard Home: GPLv3; Rethink DNS: check exact current license, avoid blindly copying code or brand names; use API/integration or build own vetted stack.
- Blocklists, malware signatures, threat intel and cloud reputations require separate redistribution/commercial entitlement review.

## 6. Commercial architecture, two Android apps and target prices

**Customer-selected target monthly prices (EUR; billing cycle assumed, to verify):**
| Product | Tier | EUR/month | Initial access / staged release plan |
|---|---|---:|---|
| AI-DeviceCare | Free | 0.00 | Security/permission posture basics, battery dashboard, limited cleaner, local diagnostics |
| AI-DeviceCare | Starter | 3.99 | Deeper battery trends, guided optimization, diagnostics/repair workflows, advanced cleaner |
| AI-DeviceCare | Pro | 6.49 | Advanced threat inspection once independently validated, extended analysis/reporting |


===== "part_29.txt" | file | SHA256 cbf0093dca7491f3dac6abfcb5c91c29be2d2046f85377d67a73ab5f5fe5667e =====
| AI-DeviceCare | Elite | 14.79 | Advanced privacy/network filtering, enhanced automation and optional cloud services with separate cost/consent policies |
| AI-DeviceCare+ | Enterprise | 29.00 | Initial org/IT-admin oriented reporting, organizational account features (scope/device count TBD) |
| AI-DeviceCare+ | Business Pro | 59.00 | Multi-device organizational analytics, management and collaboration (scope/device count TBD) |
| AI-DeviceCare+ | Business Enterprise | 129.00 | Higher-scale fleet administration, advanced policy/report exports and support (scope/device count TBD) |

**Pricing constraints:** monthly prices user-specified; no annual prices assumed; taxes, VAT, Google Play fees, data feed licenses, VPN transit/bandwidth, cloud AI usage, threat feed fees, support and per-device allowances must be costed before release. Do not misrepresent future modules as currently delivered. Business plan seat/device quotas and entitlement upgrade/downgrade semantics need product decisions.

**Critical Google Play policy:** two near-identical apps risk rejection for repetitive content. Make the second app materially different (business fleet management, organization onboarding, policy/tenant isolation, business data handling, SSO/RBAC). Validate policy before investing. Shared codebase is fine but two app targets must have distinct bundle/application IDs and differentiated experiences. If significant differentiation is not ready, release only the consumer app first and hold AI-DeviceCare+ until it is distinct and functional.

**Technical model:** monorepo, two Gradle app modules `:apps:devicecare` and `:apps:devicecare-plus`, common reusable `:core:platform`, `:core:ui`, `:core:evidence`, `:core:logging`, `:core:billing`, `:features:battery`, `:features:permissions`, `:features:diagnostics`, `:features:cleaner`, `:features:malware`, `:features:network`, `:features:fleet`; independent release channels/feature flags and backward-compatible schema. Separate Play subscription configurations per package where required, backend token validation, no unsupported assumption of automatic cross-app subscription portability.

## 6a. Milestone release train (small vertical slices, publish early)
- **M0 Foundation**: Governance, modular Kotlin/Compose build, CI, reproducible release signing, secret and supply-chain checks, permission policy gate, Play listing/privacy/data safety drafts, baseline telemetry consent. Internal release only. **Gate:** debug/release bundle reproducible, tests green, restricted permissions absent.
- **M1 Public v1.0**: Free+Starter: app & permission checks where allowed, battery level/charging/trends when available, guided battery settings, device posture and logging, limited safe cleanup with preview and user confirmation, clear boundaries. **Gate:** Play approval, Android 15 real-device QA, no silent deletions, no battery drain regression, billing purchase/restore/cancel verified.
- **M2 Pro**: Evidence-backed malware static scan + signature updates, richer diagnostics and reports. **Gate:** labeled clean/malicious validation, FPR/FNR metrics, no unverifiable detection claims, signed rule updates.
- **M3 Elite**: Single VPNService network privacy mode, DNS tracker/malware filtering, advanced opt-in automation, optional paid VPN backend when economics and commercial rights are proven. **Gate:** DNS leaks/IPv4/IPv6, conflicts with other VPNs, battery impact, policy disclosures and Play declarations.
- **M4 AI-DeviceCare+ launch**: Distinct business product: organization accounts, tenant-isolated fleet reporting, role-based access, consented enrollment and eligible Android Enterprise APIs. **Gate:** actual org-specific value differentiates from consumer offering, legal/DPA and security review, Play acceptance.
- **M5 Business tiers**: Advanced multi-device policy workflows, SLA/support, report exports, SSO and policy audit; monetize Business Pro and Business Enterprise only after measurable delivery.

**Module acceptance:** every PR must ship a scoped end-to-end vertical slice with feature gate, unit/instrumentation tests, threat model delta, battery/perf baseline, structured logs with redaction, UI accessibility (TalkBack etc.), privacy/permission and license review, test evidence. Don't let unfinished features block release of the stable earlier tier.

## 6b. Diagnostics, logging and battery requirements
- Battery signals: charging state, level, current, temperature, health enums, cycle count when available; explicitly distinguish measured values vs estimated trends and unsupported sensors.
- Battery issue triage: excessive idle drain suspicion, charging issues, thermal symptoms, power-mode settings, correlations with app foreground usage (not direct per-app mAh).
- Repair action contract: `observe -> explain -> request consent -> settings/API action -> reobserve -> report`. The OS/user retains control.
- Diagnostic event record: UUID, timestamp, module, OS/API level, device model (redacted/optional), evidence references, severity, confidence, action status, retention TTL. No raw secret content. For suspected stalkerware, do not alert the potentially monitored party from within the app or force risky removal; explain safety implications and provide discreet options.
- Battery-aware operations: event-driven plus WorkManager, rate limits; avoid VPN+AV simultaneous high-load scans and default background persistent collectors; track battery impact of AI-DeviceCare itself.
- Publish only honest capabilities; premium features with no active delivered function are not presented as buyable.

- Epic 04: Safe remediation and cleaner

- Epic 06: DNS filtering/firewall/VPN

- Epic 09: Local logging, diagnostic repair workflows and redacted export
- Epic 10: Second app AI-DeviceCare+ / differentiated business fleet and tenant isolation
Each epic should produce incremental PRs; protect main, require green unit/instrumentation/lint, SAST/SBOM, license/secret scans, Android 15 real-device QA, and compliance review. No deployment or release without explicit owner approval.
